[551] Going forward, we plan to continue to conduct balanced military exchanges with Japan, China, and Russia, centered on the ROK-US alliance

[552] Korea-Japan Relations: The two countries have gradually developed military exchanges since the establishment of the Korean Military Attaché in Japan in 1906 and the Japanese Military Attaché in Korea in

[553] Before 1990, the exchange of personnel was mainly considered, such as military personnel exchanges and exchange visits of military academy students, but since the 1990s, the Military Education and Defense Ministerial Consultation has been conducted informally since 1994, and the Korea-Japan Security Policy Consultative Meeting, in which foreign and military officials of both countries participate, has been held annually since

[554] In addition, the Defense Policy Development Conference, the Information Headquarters and each military branch's information exchange conference, and the Air Defense Practices Conference, the Japan Maritime Self-Defense Force, the Air Force and the Japan Maritime Self-Defense Force's vice-chief conference, and the Navy-Navy Conference were established

[555] In particular, in August 1999, ships and aircraft of the Japan Maritime Self-Defense Force participated in the first-ever wooden search and rescue operation in the international waters southeast of Jeju Island

[556] At the Korea-Japan Defense Ministerial Meeting held in Tokyo in May 2000, the two defense ministers shared the view that preventing the proliferation of weapons of mass destruction in the Korean Peninsula is essential for regional security, and that strengthening military exchanges between the two countries will not only enhance military trust between Korea and Japan, but also contribute to the future-oriented development of bilateral relations

[557] In addition, they agreed to regularize mutual visits between the Chairman of the Joint Chiefs of Staff of the Republic of Korea and the Chairman of the Joint Chiefs of Staff of Japan, and agreed that it would be desirable to periodically conduct joint maritime search and rescue training between the two countries going forward

[618] Through discussions on specialized instruments such as the Ball Law Transaction, we are contributing to peace and stability in the region. In terms of building trust, we are promoting agreements on the publication of a defense policy, participation in the 08 Conventional Weapons Registration System and annual security/military dialogue, exchange of high-ranking defense personnel and military training, and the Ministry of Disarmament and Non-Proliferation's 0082 0\, 2\06, 00\, etc

[619] The measures to build a lighthouse are being promoted in stages over the next 1-2 years

[625] Multilateral Security Dialogue in Northeast Asia Since 1990, the need for diverse regional security cooperation has been raised to promote stability and peace in the Northeast Asian region

[626] Our government officially proposed the "Northeast Asia Multilateral Security Dialogue" (Jibe) with the participation of Northeast Asian countries at the 411 Senior Officials' Meeting (3100-804) in May

[627] However, this proposal was not realized due to the passive stance of some countries

[628] Our government has been actively participating in efforts to prevent the proliferation of nuclear weapons by attending the NPT Review Conference in 2000, and is cooperating internationally to secure early signatures and ratification by the ratifying countries (44 countries including the five major nuclear powers, North Korea, India, and Pakistan) so that the CTBT can come into effect

[629] In addition, our government is promoting nuclear inspection activities under IAEA and dispatching experts to various fields within the Comprehensive Nuclear-Test-Ban Treaty Organization

[630] Chemical Weapons Control The Chemical Weapons Convention (2), which came into force on April 29, 1997, completely prohibits the development, production, possession, transfer, stockpiling and use of chemical weapons

[631] Countries that have joined the convention must report the contents of the convention to the Organization for the Prohibition of Chemical Weapons (OPCW) within 30 days and then accept on-site inspections

[632] As of September 23, 2000, 171 countries have signed the Convention and 139 countries have deposited their instruments of ratification

[633] Our government deposited its instrument of ratification with the United Nations on April 28, 1997, to participate in international efforts to prevent the proliferation of weapons of mass destruction and to protect the domestic chemical industry

[634] At the first General Assembly of the Parties in May of the same year, he was elected as a member of the Executive Board of the 20th General Assembly, and at the third General Assembly of the Parties in November 1998, he assumed the position of Chairman and carried out his duties until the fourth General Assembly of the Parties in

[635] Following the 2nd, 3rd and 4th Conferences of the Parties, at the 5th Conference of the Parties held in May 2000, we also emphasized that in order to eliminate the chemical weapons threat through North Korea's accession to the 0\0 Convention, all countries in the world suspected of possessing chemical weapons must join the Convention, and that 0ㅁ20\ must make efforts to this end

[636] proposed a 'universality resolution' and passed it unanimously

[637] Meanwhile, four Korean consular officers are working at the OPCW headquarters as high-ranking officers and international inspectors

[638] In addition, the chemical analysis laboratory of the Agency for Defense Development (ADD) was designated as an 'authorized chemical analysis laboratory' in November 1998 and is carrying out its duties

[639] Biological Weapons Control The Biological Weapons Convention (BWC), which entered into force on March 26, 1975, has 145 countries as of September 2000 (South Korea: June 1987, North Korea: March

[640] However, as there is currently no verification system to monitor the implementation of 8\0, member countries are holding meetings of government experts, special groups (^0 1100 8『040) and focusing on discussing ways to establish a verification system

[641] In accordance with the final declaration of the 4th Review Conference on December 8, 1996, which implied the adoption of the Verification Protocol at the 5th Review Conference (2001), the Western group including the United States, the United Kingdom, Japan, and Australia, including our country, are accelerating negotiations with the goal of concluding the Verification Protocol by the end of 2000 or early 2001 at the latest

[642] However, negotiations are facing difficulties due to differences in positions between the Western group and non-aligned countries on some issues, such as the selection of reporting targets and agents, the visitation and inspection system, and technological cooperation, so there is a possibility of delay

[643] Our country is actively responding to the international community's efforts to control the proliferation of biological weapons, including by sending a government delegation comprised of personnel from the Geneva Mission, the Ministry of Foreign Affairs and Trade, the Ministry of National Defense, the Ministry of Trade, Industry and Energy, and research institutes under the Ministry of Health and Welfare to special group meetings and evaluation meetings

[644] Missile Control Today, the concept of weapons of mass destruction has expanded to include not only nuclear and chemical weapons, but also the missiles that carry them

[645] In that sense, missile control is a very important part of controlling weapons of mass destruction

[646] Currently, the only international missile control system is the 'Missile Technology Control System' 641040) which is based on voluntary export control by member countries. 00'0 was launched in 1987 when seven advanced countries including the United States and France adopted guidelines to impose specific controls on the inter-country transfer and final use of missiles with a range of 30010 and a warhead of 500 or more, as well as related parts and technology. Currently, 32 countries are participating in the meeting

[647] It is considered to have made a significant contribution to preventing and slowing the proliferation of offensive missiles without restricting the civilian aerospace industry

[648] Our government hopes to join MTCR, and although not yet a member, we have already joined various treaties and agreements on the non-proliferation of weapons of mass destruction, and have been complying with the MTCR guidelines by reflecting them in the 'Strategic Materials Export/Import Notice' since

[649] Our government's will and efforts demonstrate that Korea is fully qualified to become a member of the MTCR

[650] Conventional Arms Control As the number of casualties from the killing of innocent civilians by conventional weapons in recent international conflict zones has increased, the need for conventional arms control has become increasingly urgent worldwide

[651] In particular, international regulation discussions on small arms and landmines are being held by the United Nations, international organizations, and non-governmental organizations. -104- --- Page

[652] The Ministry of Finance has been participating in the 044 'Jaeri (digit 681566『 Lee 000760110081 485939)' which has been implemented for UN member countries since 1993, and is registered at the tax office

[653] The status of exports and imports of other weapon systems is regularly reported to 10 C every year

[654] In addition, we participated in the Wassenaar Arrangement, which was launched in 1997, to prevent indiscriminate transfer of conventional weapons and dual-use items and technologies, and are implementing export controls by reflecting the controlled items in the “Public Notice on Export and Import of Strategic Materials”

[655] Meanwhile, as civilian casualties from the landmines have become more serious, international public opinion has been formed that landmines should be regulated, and Protocol II (aka the Protocol on the Prohibition of the Use of Landmines) to the Convention on Certain Conventional Weapons, which was concluded in 1980, was revised in May

[656] The draft strictly regulates the use of landmines, including prohibiting the use of undetectable anti-personnel mines and mandating the use of self-detonating and self-disabling devices

[657] However, some countries, including Canada, judged that the Protocol II Second Annex Protocol was insufficient to fundamentally prevent the damage and adopted the Ottawa Convention

[658] The Ottawa Convention entered into force on March 1, 1999, and as of September 2000, 139 countries around the world had signed it and 107 had ratified it

[659] The government agrees with the purpose of a total ban on anti-personnel landmines on humanitarian grounds, but given the current special security situation, it is difficult to immediately and completely stop the use of highly effective defensive landmines and dispose of landmines in a short period of time. Therefore, the government cannot immediately participate in the Ottawa Convention, and its official position is that it can only join after alternatives to anti-personnel landmines are available

[660] We are currently pursuing a more flexible specific 'conventional weapons (20\)' centered around 10*, with the goal of joining within 2000, in order to secure international cooperation and technical support for mine removal after unification

[661] The government is working to expand the international community's understanding and support for the fact that the use of anti-personnel landmines is exceptional due to the special security situation on the Korean Peninsula, while also making efforts to acquire anti-personnel landmines in multiple stages

[684] In addition, the operating expenses required to improve the welfare of soldiers and to promote future-oriented projects such as military informatization and science in line with the improvement of the people's standard of living are continuously increasing

[685] Moreover, establishing a strong security posture is an essential element for effectively pursuing the government's North Korea policy, so appropriate defense spending should be given priority consideration at the national strategic level

[686] Nevertheless, the defense budget has continued to decline as a percentage of the national economy and government finances since the 1990s, reaching only 2.7% of the national economy and 16.7% of the government finances (general account) in

[687] Even looking at the growth rate of defense spending over the past five years, it is only half the growth rate of government finances due to the impact of the financial crisis in

[688] Additionally, the real purchasing power of defense spending, taking into account inflation and exchange rate increases, is estimated to be at the level of the 1980s

[689] ※ - 112 - --- Page 113 --- Our country's defense budget burden ratio (2.7/30) is by no means a high level compared to its economic power

[691] Day 29 29 80 Book Delivery & Defense Security Committee ※ The ratio of defense spending to government finance is based on general accounting

[692] The burden ratio in 1995, 1996, 1997, 1998, 1999, and 2000 averaged 11.8%, which is about four times our level, and even countries with almost no party cooperation, such as Singapore, spend 6.0% on defense

[693] Under these circumstances, our military is making various efforts, including regular cost-cutting efforts and innovation in defense management, to generate maximum power with the given resources, but it is not enough to build a massive military force capable of simultaneously preparing for current and future threats

[694] In particular, considering the characteristics of military force construction that require a lead time of more than 10 years for the deployment of advanced weapon systems, training of specialized personnel, and improvement of military structure, at least 3% of the national defense budget must be secured in a long-term and stable manner in order to smoothly promote the mid- to long-term military force construction plan

[695] Korea Institute for Defense Analyses, 「Mid- to Long-Term Defense Expenditure Requirement Judgment,, 「Establishment of Mid- to Long-Term Defense Available Resources Judgment System」, etc. --- Page 116 --- Section 2 Development of Defense Acquisition Work System In order to secure transparency in weapons procurement projects, the system has been improved several times and in 1999, an institutional device was established to fundamentally secure transparency in defense acquisition projects

[696] In order for public companies in the business promotion process to pursue military acquisition-related projects such as weapon system development, production, and bidding, it is essential to secure information on acquisition projects. However, since most of it is classified as secret, there have been problems such as frequent military secret leaks or suspicions of preferential treatment

[697] To address these issues and ensure transparency, the Ministry of National Defense established the system in

[698] The classification criteria for monthly defense acquisition projects have been significantly relaxed and improved

[699] Accordingly, the principle was to classify matters related to the defense acquisition project, which had been classified as secret until now, as general, and to disclose the equipment name, total quantity, project period, general performance and specifications, quantity by type, and total funds

[700] However, essential fields such as strategic non-profit weapon projects and research and development trials are classified as confidential, but when the level of financial support is reduced to the maximum extent possible, confidential documents such as mid-term plans are separately prepared for public release and placed in designated locations such as the Procurement Headquarters and Defense Committee to be disclosed to related companies and organizations. 1 Clear establishment of decision-making criteria and procedures and advance notice The ability to decide on the aircraft type, decide on the method of recruitment, and select a company is the most important decision-making procedure in the process of introducing a subsidiary

[701] Even if you do your best to promote a business fairly and without specific standards and procedures, there is a structural problem in that those who fail will continue to raise distrust and suspicion

[702] To fundamentally resolve these issues, the evaluation requirements and criteria required for various decision-making processes were notified to participating companies in advance, and the evaluation and decision-making results were made public. This system is being applied to all decision-making processes that require comparative evaluation, such as deciding on aircraft types and delivery methods, trial evaluations, and company selection

[703] These evaluation factors, criteria and procedures are prepared by the business management department, but are finalized through open discussions with the participation of experts from relevant departments and institutions such as the Ministry of National Defense, the Command and Staff, the Small and Medium Business Administration, the Korea Institute for Defense Analyses, and the Agency for Defense Development, and are announced in advance to participating companies through requests for proposals

[704] Preventing suspicions of overpriced purchases and budget waste by strengthening the ability to verify cost information Most suspicions of budget waste due to overpriced purchases of weapons arise from a lack of specialized analysis and verification capabilities regarding cost/price information

[705] To dispel such suspicions, a unified cost/price information collection, analysis, accumulation, distribution, and utilization system was established and cost analysis was conducted

[706] In addition, we have strengthened our cost/price information analysis and verification capabilities by having the Korea Institute for Defense Analyses 06104 conduct a professional cost analysis

[707] Information exchange between companies and the military is fostered. The exchange of information between companies and the military is weak in terms of official channels for information exchange, and the military culture of tabooing contact with companies limits opportunities for companies to promote themselves or participate in business, which raises issues of fairness and suspicions of preferential treatment for certain companies

[708] Accordingly, a channel was created to exchange information between companies and the military so that companies can legitimately provide information to the military and the military can freely request information from companies

[709] Information requests and collection are mutually exchanged through the Internet homepage, and when necessary, data collection is formalized by publicizing a standard form for requesting/collecting information

[710] In addition, we are activating information exchange by utilizing various opportunities such as the company's weapon product/technology information briefing sessions

[712] Accordingly, investment projects are promoted with a focus on converting foreign procurement projects to domestic procurement as much as possible to improve domestic employment stability and the operating rate of defense industry companies, and contributing to revitalizing the domestic economy by executing the budget early. [724] Research and Development Research and Development projects are planned to develop capabilities to develop equipment that are suitable for Korean conditions, and major system development projects include development of fighter external electronic warfare equipment, military tactical data distribution system 0<47128), 8th Air Force air defense command and control system 040830), automated system unmanned aerial vehicles, remote chemical automatic warning aircraft, etc. Small unmanned aerial vehicles (44\) Section 4 Building domestic defense industry foundation

[725] Defense research and development of advanced weapons in Korea has been actively pursued since the establishment of the Agency for Defense Development (ADD) in 1970, and has contributed to establishing an independent national defense system by domestically producing most conventional weapons over the past 30 years

[726] However, as a result of focusing on domestic production of conventional weapons, we are still dependent on advanced countries for advanced core technology fields

[727] Accordingly, in order to secure the capability to develop Korean style cutting-edge weapons to prepare for the future security environment, our military has set the goal of national defense science and technology policy as 'entering the advanced tier of national defense science and technology by 2015' and has established and is consistently promoting the 'Five Major Policies for the Advancement of National Defense Science and Technology

[728] First, we plan to select cutting-edge core technologies required for future warfare and gradually advance them to the level of advanced countries

[729] In 1999, the "Defense and Technology Plan" was published, and 14 key weapon systems and 31 core technologies were selected to be intensively developed at a strategic level, thereby establishing a foundation for securing the capability to develop future cutting-edge weapons with new technologies and new technologies

[730] In addition, by transitioning to foundation formation, concentration, and selective leap forward through gradual concentrated investment and development, we have secured cutting-edge technologies with international competitiveness, and in other fields, we have gradually expanded general technology fields such as civil-military dual-use technologies and common base technologies

[731] Page 121 --- [Figure 3-7] Step-by-step leap forward concept _ Foundation formation ㆍ Concentration ㆍ Separate leap forward ㆍ (Step 1: 2005) (Step 2: 2010) (Step 3: 2015) Military-only strategic world-class core core technology field Concentrated technology field Leap forward technology field Power system > Float | Implementation Second, it is to acquire core technologies at low cost and with high efficiency

[732] To this end, various technology acquisition models were applied according to the level or stage of technology development goals to maximize research effectiveness

[733] In particular, by expanding the implementation of the "mid-entry" technology development method, which introduces core technologies in the early stages from advanced countries and initiates practical application and improvement development, the development period and budget were drastically reduced

[734] Third, we must actively utilize the nation's comprehensive science and technology base to achieve efficient technology acquisition

[735] To this end, the Ministry of National Defense plans to actively participate in the policy-making of the National Science and Technology Committee in order to promote defense science and technology linked to national science and technology policy

[736] In addition, in order to systematically conduct basic research centered on academia, five specialized research centers have been designated and operated at universities and government research institutes since 1994, thereby solidifying the industry-academia-research cooperation system

[737] Since the enactment of the Civilian-Military Dual-Use Technology Promotion Act in April 1998, the Civilian-Military Dual-Use Technology Development Project has been jointly promoted by four government ministries in four key projects: technology development, technology transfer, standardization, and technology information exchange

[738] In relation to this, after promoting the project for "10 pilot projects" over a period of 9 years from 1997, the scale has been gradually expanded, such as promoting purchases for a total of 47 projects in

[739] Fourth, we will strengthen the planning and management system focused on technology development

[740] To this end, the entire process of core technology development, including planning, planning, development, application, and feedback, was promoted consistently, and the developed technology was not simply accumulated as technical data, but was directly utilized in all processes. By establishing the Branch in Lock Defense Science and Technology 00, a comprehensive management system in the form of knowledge management was established

[741] Lastly, in order to foster and support long-term defense research and development, we plan to gradually increase investment in research and development from the current 5% of defense spending to 10% by

[742] In addition, we plan to build a scientific and technological foundation by securing excellent scientific and technological professionals, expanding research facilities, and strengthening the information infrastructure

[743] In particular, in order to promote intensive research and development on cutting-edge core technologies required for future warfare, the Agency for Defense Development was reformed in March 2000 to establish its status as a specialized research institute for the development of cutting-edge weapon systems and core technologies

[744] In addition, we are improving research performance by introducing and implementing a variety of incentive systems such as research and development grants and performance-based pay for researchers. Through these future-oriented policy initiatives, our military is securing its own core technologies and securing the ability to research and develop Korean-style cutting-edge weapons, thereby further solidifying the foundation of independent national defense as an advanced defense science and technology powerhouse of the 91st century. Securing competitiveness of the defense industry and strengthening its foundation Since the enactment of the ‘Special Measures Act on the Defense Industry’ in 1973, our defense industry succeeded in domestically producing some conventional weapons in the mid-1980s, and has also contributed to the development of domestic civilian industries in areas such as precision machinery and heavy chemical industries

[745] Currently, approximately 80 defense industry companies are producing approximately 330 types of defense products, and sales have reached approximately 3.4 trillion won, achieving quantitative growth

[746] However, since the 1990s, while the demand for advanced weapon systems has increased and the development of weapon systems and the internationalization of the defense industry have progressed, the domestic demand for basic weaponry has decreased, resulting in the defense industry's operating rate remaining at around 50%, increasing the difficulties faced by companies

[747] Accordingly, the Ministry of National Defense is continuously supplementing and developing the specialization and series system and the defense industry development support policy to secure the competitiveness of the defense industry and strengthen its foundation

[748] In January 1999, the Ministry of National Defense revised the 'Special Measures Act on the Defense Industry' to expand the participation of excellent companies with technological capabilities in the defense industry

[749] In addition, fields with high compatibility with the civilian sector in terms of production facilities and technology were converted to a competitive system, while fields requiring large-scale facility investment and low military demand were converted to a dedicated system

[750] Accordingly, through an on-site inspection by a government-wide expert evaluation team, 19 fields, which account for 40% of 47 specialized fields, and 147 items, which account for 61% of 241 affiliated items, were converted to a competitive system. In addition, the number of specialized companies was reduced from 172 to 86, and the number of affiliated items and industries was drastically reorganized

[751] In particular, 29 items among the series items were designated as items protected for small and medium-sized enterprises, limiting the participation of large corporations and allowing competition only among small and medium-sized enterprises

[752] Supplementary support for the development of the defense industry. In order to develop and support the defense industry, the Ministry of National Defense established a defense industry development fund of 118.6 billion won from 1980 to 1996 and loaned a total of 391.1 billion won to defense companies for the raw material stockpiling project and research and development launch fund for producing defense products

[753] In 2000, we plan to provide 42.5 billion won to 24 companies for raw material reserves and research and development funds, and we plan to raise additional defense industry promotion funds annually to reach the target amount of 3,000 billion won early and expand the support scale. In addition, we are continuously developing the defense product tax support system so that value-added tax, special consumption tax, and customs duties can be exempted or reduced in order to reduce the burden of defense spending due to various taxes and alleviate the financial difficulties of defense companies

[754] And in March 1999, the "Enforcement Decree of the Defense Industry Special Measures Act" was revised to allow for the removal of idle defense equipment upon application by defense companies in cases where idle defense equipment is causing an excessive burden on management

[755] Meanwhile, in order to facilitate the smooth restructuring of domestic companies and the attraction of foreign capital after the financial crisis, foreign investment in domestic defense industry companies is permitted in principle to the extent that it does not significantly affect national security

[756] Strengthening International Defense Industry Cooperation International defense industry cooperation focuses on enhancing our defense industry competitiveness by strengthening international cooperation among defense industries and discovering and expanding potential areas for cooperation such as joint production and cooperative exports

[757] In addition, in order to ensure long-term stable operation throughout the entire life cycle of introduced weapons, the Ministry of National Defense has concluded institutional agreements such as quality assurance, price information exchange, and technical data exchange agreements. In the meantime, the Ministry of National Defense has diversified defense industry cooperation from the previous US-centered to Europe, Asia, and South America by concluding cooperation agreements with the US, Turkey, Indonesia, and Venezuela, and plans to further strengthen defense industry cooperation with target countries for defense industry exports in the future

[758] Meanwhile, various regulations were relaxed to support defense industry exports, and support for export consultations and marketing activities by overseas military attachés was strengthened

[759] Recently, the qualification for exporting defense products has been relaxed from the previous licensing system to a reporting system, a legal basis has been established to support military equipment and facilities when exporting defense products, and military diplomacy activities with major defense export target countries have been strengthened. The defense exhibition hall installed on the 2nd floor of the War Memorial of Korea has contributed to the discovery of potential projects and expansion of exports

[760] In 2001, the Air Force - The 3rd Seoul International Air Show, which is scheduled to be jointly hosted by the Defense Industry Promotion Association, and the land and sea defense industry, is expected to be held

[761] The six-arms equipment will also be exhibited simultaneously to promote the excellence of domestically produced defense products to the world

It is being built in stages, focusing on essential functions, taking into account the characteristics of each military and battlefield function

[782] The Ground Tactical O4I System is a system that automates the battlefield functions of tactical units below the corps, enabling real-time command and control while checking the battlefield. A pilot system was built and operated in one department in 1999, and as a result, performance improvement and system expansion were carried out in stages with a focus on essential functions. The Navy Tactical O4I System was developed to expand the operational area of the Naval Tactical Data System O4I currently in operation in some areas and reinforce its function to grasp the maritime situation in real time and to support integrated maritime operations. It was also developed into a Tactical O4I System that enables joint operations with the Army and Air Force. [799] Section 4: Construction of a National Defense Information and Communications Infrastructure System that Guarantees Real-Time Information Distribution The National Defense Information and Communications Infrastructure System is being promoted in stages as a system that connects and integrates strategic and information communication networks connected to the high-speed information network and satellite network into a grid network to ensure distribution of information for warfare and national defense operations. According to the plan, a wide-area information and communication network (\^4) and a local information and communication network (.ㅅ피) will be constructed in units at the regiment level or higher by the end of 2000, and asynchronous transmission mode (&40) switches will be installed in major units to ensure the distribution of large amounts of military information

[800] And the construction of the National Defense Information and Communication Network is planned to be completed by 2005, by establishing a common operating system that guarantees the flow of high-band information that can guarantee the joint combat power and the linkage and interoperability between the Army, Navy, and Air Force, and by completing the common new infrastructure that can flow a large amount of military information at high speed in real time

[828] The military is pursuing policies to ensure job stability so that soldiers can focus on their service and to ensure that military families and discharged soldiers can live a stable life. Improvement of treatment of career soldiers Improvement of treatment of the military will follow the government's plan to improve treatment of civil servants, but in order to guarantee the special nature and occupation of military service, we plan to promote the establishment of university student allowances and separation allowances, and make unconstitutional, aircraft, ship, contact (special) area, and incentive allowances a reality

[829] Over the past nine years, some allowances have been cut or frozen due to the ups and downs of the national economy, but in 2000, basic pay and family allowances were raised and an additional salary adjustment allowance was introduced in the second half of the year to narrow the wage gap with private companies

[830] Improvement of military housing policy and measures for home ownership Most of the soldiers are unable to lead stable lives for themselves and their families due to work in remote areas or moving frequently, so no matter where they are, we are actively securing military residences and promoting home ownership so that they can solve their housing problems. As of the end of 1999, 97.3% of the military residences were in possession (67,528 households) compared to the demand (69,374 households), but we plan to secure 100% by

[831] However, there are 6,000 households, or 9.0%, of official residences built more than 25 years ago, and there are 110,240 households of small official residences of 13 pyeong or less

[832] Since 2000, the military has been expanding the number of official residences in complexes by region and city in areas with good transportation, culture, and education conditions, using a national support budget and its own funds to improve these aging and cramped official residences. The military also plans to actively expand purchases rather than construction to ensure flexibility in responding to future military restructuring

[833] In addition, we are diversifying the balance (34/28/39 pyeong) by number of cohabiting families, gender, and age distribution, and are striving to improve the quality of the living environment

[835] The current situation is far below 3%

[836] Accordingly, the military is supplying more than 2,000 houses annually through the Military Mutual Aid Association to expand opportunities for career soldiers to own their own homes and is supporting homebuyer loans

[837] Improving the living conditions of military families

[838] The military tax exemption system was established to comprehensively support the stable living conditions of career soldiers. However, compared to the early days of implementation, the actual tax exemption benefits have decreased due to factors such as a reduction in the tax exemption rate and the abolition of the special consumption tax on home appliances (excluding air conditioners), and the system is now at a blurred level

[839] To improve this, we have significantly expanded the duty-free volume of consumer goods, and are working with relevant organizations to ensure that durable goods receive practical duty-free benefits

[840] Due to the nature of military service, professional soldiers face many restrictions on their education, such as working in frontline or isolated areas, moving frequently, and living separately. The military is promoting the expansion of dormitory facilities for children of military personnel and the expansion of special admissions. Currently, 1,868 children are accommodated in 19 dormitories in 10 major cities across the country. In 2000, an additional facility that can accommodate 100 people will be built in the Daejeon Jaun area. After 2001, the military plans to re-evaluate the additional needs and expand the construction of dormitories for children of military personnel

[841] In addition, through the special admission system, 187 children of military service were accepted to 13 universities, including Korea University and Sogang University, in 2000, and discussions are underway with relevant ministries and universities to expand the number of special admission universities to 39 in

Securing Reserve Forces Funds and Promoting Profitable Businesses The Military Affairs Research Institute was established in 1994 to support military research activities of reserve forces

[851] The Military Affairs Research Institute is expanding its funds through profitable projects such as investments in excellent venture companies

[852] Supplementary measures for the bonus points system for military service members The bonus points system for military service members was established in 1961 and has been in effect for nearly 40 years. It was created to compensate for individual rights and boost morale in cases of interruption of studies and livelihood, and disadvantages in employment conditions due to military service

[853] However, on December 23, 1999, the Constitutional Court ruled that this system was unconstitutional on the grounds that it infringed on the rights of women and those exempted from military service to hold public office

[854] After this decision, the backlash from discharged soldiers has intensified, and the morale of active-duty soldiers has declined as negative perceptions of the sacred duty of military service have spread

[855] Accordingly, the Ministry of National Defense is collecting opinions from relevant ministries and is selecting and promoting seven supplementary measures under the leadership of the Ministry of Patriots and Veterans Affairs

[856] The 6 items that have been reviewed and are being implemented or revised are: ㆍ Extension of the upper age limit for taking the 07 civil service examination by 3 years, @Preferential hiring when passing the civil service examination, @Recognition of military service period, @Pay grade calculation, etc. have been finalized as bills, and the related law, the 'Veteran Recognition Act' will be revised and implemented by the end of 2000. ㆍ Strengthening vocational training for discharged soldiers - The Ministry of National Defense will independently activate vocational training and information technology education, etc. - Reflected in the vocational ability development plan for vulnerable groups, etc. under the Ministry of Employment and Labor, and in implementation. ㆍTuition loan support for students returning from military service will be given priority for loans only, as interest-free or low-interest loans are deemed difficult in consideration of budgetary conditions and fairness with other students. ㅇ @National social service activity bonus points system is currently being studied and reviewed by the Ministry of Patriots and Veterans Affairs as a long-term task due to insufficient social infrastructure creation following implementation

[857] Most countries today are maintaining their standing forces as responsive forces while increasing the power of their reserve forces

[858] In order to maintain a large standing force with limited national finances, there will be many restrictions on military spending, so dependence on reserve forces is expected to increase further

[859] In particular, since the future war is expected to be a total war in which all of the country's resources will be mobilized, the efficient mobilization and execution of reserve forces in times of emergency will determine the outcome of the war

[860] Therefore, our military is promoting mobilization tasks with an emphasis on the refinement of reserve forces and the establishment of a readiness posture for immediate mobilization in order to foster reserve forces capable of immediate deployment

[861] First, in accordance with the military organization chart for each district, town, and district, the Military Manpower Administration's mobilization enforcement officers were reinforced and mobilization deployment locations were adjusted to facilitate rapid transport, strengthening mobilization execution capabilities. Second, by comparing and analyzing available resources against mobilization requirements, allocation areas were adjusted and the scope of resource utilization by military specialty was adjusted to ensure efficient mobilization

[862] Third, by only designating replacements for unsuitable specialists or those with overtime, when designated for mobilization, we aimed to improve the combat power of the unit by fixing the unit and position, and resolved complaints about annual changes in the unit and position when designated for mobilization. Fourth, we developed a program that can determine the need for loss replacement, and established a reasonable calculation system. -141- --- Page 142 --- 으 10 요 조 모 > ㅁ 0브 아8 띠0 @뜨 4 0! 요 9 The effectiveness of the operation plan was guaranteed and the burden of running a wartime farm was relieved. [898] Over the past eight years, we have focused on short-term and long-term tasks such as restructuring the national defense organization and military, improving defense capabilities, reforming the personnel and education systems, and innovating defense management

[899] Going forward, we will focus all our capabilities on “military innovation and military informatization” to accelerate military reform more fundamentally and build a 21st-century advanced elite national defense force

[900] The mid-term defense goals until 2008 are to lay the foundation for achieving the long-term defense goals. From then on, we will pursue innovative defense development that transcends an era. The goals of the defense policy to be achieved by 2003 are as follows

[901] First, a 'strong elite military' with high morale and well-established discipline; second, a 'professional military' brimming with pride and a strong sense of mission; third, a 'military that operates efficiently' through management innovation and informatization; and fourth, we are pursuing these national defense sentiment priorities as follows, and in the long term, we will focus on establishing a future national defense environment and concept and fostering an advanced elite military that can create a strong national defense force at a low cost through military innovation in each field and scientific and informatized national defense

[902] First, reorganization of the military structure into an economical one suited to Korean conditions. Second, improvement of defense capabilities in a goal-oriented and transparent manner. Third, development of a rational personnel and education system and improvement of the military service system into a fair one. Fourth, innovation of defense management for efficient resource management. The Defense Reform Promotion Committee was launched on April 15, 1998 as an organization directly under the Minister and is responsible for proactively promoting the eight-year defense reform

[903] In parallel, each military branch established a reform promotion committee to ensure linked reforms between the Ministry of National Defense and each military branch. The Defense Reform Promotion Committee established the "Five-Year Defense Reform Plan" and, after receiving the President's approval on July 8, 1998, began full-scale defense reform, achieving the success of completing 45 out of 58 tasks by August

[904] In addition, in April 1999, the "Military Innovation Planning Group" was established within the Defense Reform Promotion Council to plan long-term future national defense plans. The group analyzes the future security environment and national development trends for 20 to 30 years in the future to derive national defense strategies, and researches basic concepts and directions for various fields such as battlefield operations, military doctrine, military techniques, force systems, operational systems, leadership, and education and training suitable for our military

[905] In addition, in order to coordinate and control the work of the Defense Information Technology Advisory Committee with greater intensity, the Defense Reform Promotion Committee Chairman was appointed to the position of Minister for Planning and Organization of the General Affairs of the Defense Reform Promotion Committee. -152- --- Page 153 --- Coordination and Organization of the Military's Upper Functions for Efficient and Economical Reform (see Defense Policy Record 39 for the organization of the Ministry of National Defense). [927] The Ministry of National Defense has established a comprehensive management system to strongly promote defense information technology by separating the Information Planning Office from the Acquisition Office and reorganizing it into an organization directly under the Vice Minister. [930] The regulatory nature of the military supply company registration system and the company status investigation system were abolished, and the number of companies and items subject to military supply safety diagnosis was drastically reduced from 1,235 to 361, thereby opening up the military supply sector to allow many private companies to participate under the principle of free competition

[972] Section 3 Innovation in Defense Research and Development Defense science and technology capabilities are the core foundation of military power

[973] In the 21st century, the information age, the outcome of war will be determined by qualitative capabilities based on advanced technology rather than the quantitative scale of military power

[974] Therefore, in order to effectively prepare for future warfare, securing independent defense science and technology capabilities is essential

[975] The introduction of advanced power from overseas is not only a huge economic burden, but also has limitations due to the increasingly stringent regulations and controls on technology transfer in advanced countries

[976] Since the 1970s, we have been making multifaceted efforts to expand the defense science and technology base under the banner of independent national defense

[977] Nevertheless, the reality today is that most major equipment and supplies are purchased from advanced countries, and the technological gap with advanced countries is still very low

[978] This is because the previous power generation method has focused on overseas production

[979] As the war paradigm of the times develops, the concept of military power construction is also changing

[980] We must secure the ability to participate in advanced countries by fundamentally innovating software from the previous "hardwarecentric" approach

[981] Research and development innovation is about redesigning all systems, institutions, and procedures with a focus on securing future cutting-edge technologies and strengthening the defense industry foundation

[982] To this end, the following innovation directions were established

[983] First, we will build an advanced country in conjunction with the national science and technology strategy

[984] Second, we will select future cutting-edge core technologies and prioritize their development

[985] Third, we will upgrade some key core technologies to world-class standards

[986] Fourth, comparison: Boldly utilize advanced technologies that have an edge

[987] Fifth, to have international competitiveness in the defense industry 0 to 10 mu 0 si na tamen 8 1 of 4 06 when po 0 6 nyo pi ui o er 08 e (u 9 16 so to 2 dum tu i (eun also dae: -163- --- Page 164 --- In order to promote these tasks, a 'Research and Development Innovation Team' was newly formed in the 'Military Innovation Planning Group' (May 2000), and in the future, a new defense research and development and defense industry paradigm will be established and developed and made into policies and plans

[988] Section 4. The rapid development of military science and technology in preparation for future warfare is a part of the war paradigm of the 31st century. The appearance of precision strike and information paralysis warfare in the information age 1 is partially seen in the Gulf War and the Kosovo War. Stealth transport systems, sensors, computers and the new network are emerging in the era of mass destruction warfare. 8 Su Go Pu Po Kojul 1 Lee 02 8 ㅋㅇ0 |” Go Go Jump Eunbi cyclically developed. As battlefield visualization and information sharing capabilities are developed, the zone's three dimensions of land, sea and air will expand to space and cyberspace, and overall capabilities will be greatly improved

[989] In particular, it will have a significant impact on the outcome of the dispute, as it can protect or paralyze the information system

[990] In the current circumstances, new military innovations are being pursued

[991] The United States is developing a new power system using [military innovation] technology, and dramatically increasing combat capabilities by effectively innovating operational concepts and organizational formation. The United States is pioneering and developing an information and knowledge-based operational paradigm by utilizing information and communication, robotics, and microelectromechanical technologies

[1003] The Military Manpower Administration, which is implementing the real-name military service system, enacted and implemented the 'Act on Reporting and Disclosure of Military Service History of Public Officials, etc.' on May 24, 1999, which mandates reporting of military service history for elected public officials, high-ranking public officials, and their direct descendants

[1004] A person with a duty to report must, within one month from the date of acquiring the relevant position, report the military service history of himself and his immediate subordinates aged 18 or older to the head of his/her affiliated organization by attaching supporting documents such as a military service certificate. The head of the affiliated organization that received the report must notify the Military Manpower Administration within one month

[1005] The contents of the report from my reporting agency will be summarized and re-disclosed within one month from the date of receipt of the report

[1006] Meanwhile, if there is a change in military service information after the initial report, a regular change report must be submitted by the end of January of the following year based on the 1st of each year, and the disclosure procedure for this is as follows

[1007] However, for public office candidates and those who hold public office requiring the consent of the National Assembly, the chairman of the election management committee in the relevant constituency and the speaker of the National Assembly are each responsible for disclosing. In relation to this, on October 29, 1999, the military service history of a total of 12,674 people, including 5,885 people required to report and 6,789 of their direct descendants out of the approximately 6,000 positions subject to disclosure as stipulated in the same Act, was disclosed for the first time, and by September 2000, a total of 13,965 people (including 7,413 direct descendants) had been disclosed

[1008] The conscription physical examination, which was conducted by active-duty military doctors until the implementation of the dedicated medical doctor system, has been implemented under the dedicated medical doctor system since

[1009] A conscription specialist doctor is selected from among specialist doctors who are not on active duty and works as a contract civil servant under the Military Manpower Administration, specializing in conscription physical examinations

[1010] There are 111 full-time doctors required for the 11 conscription physical examination teams across the country, but 71 doctors were assigned to 7 teams until 2000, and from 2001, all full-time doctors will be conducting physical examinations for soldiers

[1011] The "Physical Rating Decision Review Committee" established within the Military Manpower Administration is responsible for reviewing system improvements and inquiries related to physical rating decisions, and the Agricultural and Welfare Committee established within each regional Military Manpower Administration deliberates on individual military service dispositions

[1012] The subjects of deliberation by the Regional Military Manpower Administration Committee are those who were judged to be grade 5 or 6 based on the results of the physical examination, and those who passed the physical examination and raised objections to the results

[1013] The committee's decision is based on the consensus of all members of the deliberation committee, and if there is disagreement, a detailed examination at a military hospital will be conducted

[1014] In addition, the entire deliberation process was made public to the family of the subject of deliberation, and they were allowed to express their opinions. When necessary, representatives of the family of a person with military service obligation and observers of military administration briefing sessions were also appointed as members of the deliberation committee

[1015] In addition, in order to further enhance the credibility of the deliberation, ‘family doctors’ have been appointed as members of the deliberation committee since

[1016] In 1999, 12,784 people who were exempted were referred to the review committee, 12,558 were found guilty, and 296 were ordered to undergo a detailed physical examination at a military hospital

[1017] The rational adjustment of the alternative service system The value of the alternative service system is emphasized in that it increases the contribution to the nation and society through the efficient distribution of military service resources

[1018] Accordingly, in order to make this system more active, the service type and service field were reasonably adjusted

[1019] First, for those eligible for public service workers with higher education, the order of the previous conscription physical examination year will be the earliest and the order of age will be the oldest. Accordingly, in the future, in areas with a backlog of candidates for conscription, cases where those with lower education are not conscripted first will be prevented

[1020] Also, in order to prevent cases of accidents occurring frequently while serving as public service workers and causing social trouble, a lower priority system for the order of call-up for public service workers was newly established. The order of call-up was adjusted to lower priority for those who were sentenced to imprisonment or a heavier sentence, including those with suspended sentences (excluding those who violated the Special Act on the Handling of Traffic Accidents and the Road Traffic Act), those who have been in juvenile detention centers, those with mental illness, and those with a physical grade of 4 due to tattoos, self-harm, or scars

[1021] In addition, efforts were made to improve the efficiency of the operation of specialized research/industrial functional personnel

[1022] In cases where a specialized researcher/industrial functional agent was not working in the relevant field at the time of transfer, the transfer was cancelled. However, in cases where the violation was not intentional or was due to an unfair work order from the head of the designated company and the case was minor, the service period was extended for the period of the violation instead of the transfer cancellation

[1034] For those who study abroad before the age of 17, overseas travel was permitted only if they attached a certificate of overseas study approval or a certificate of special study abroad from the Minister of Education. [1035] In addition, the period of permission for overseas students to travel abroad, which was set at 8 years, was extended until the expected graduation date, and the departure report at the military service report office at airports and ports for those who have completed military service or are exempted from military service (8th National Service) when leaving the country was abolished

[1036] And those who wish to undergo a priority conscription examination for reasons such as wanting to travel abroad before the conscription examination in their city, county, or district of residence begins or wanting to be quickly assigned to industrial functional personnel, can submit a “priority conscription examination application” by the day before the conscription examination they wish to have. Innovation in military service administration: Promotion of ‘Military Service Administration’. The Military Manpower Administration has established the ‘Military Service Administration 9100 21 Plan’ to create a new military service culture in the new millennium, including fair military service administration, efficient resource management, and quality resource recruitment, and is promoting innovation in military service

[1037] Accordingly, we will gradually promote advanced military administration by improving related systems and procedures and developing new information systems

[1041] Future Plan Starting in 2001, the entire conscription examination process will be completely computerized to ensure fairness and transparency in the conscription examination process and prevent military service corruption. [1044] Meanwhile, the plan is to establish a "Central Physical Examination Center of the Military Manpower Administration" to centralize the physical examinations currently being conducted in two parts by the Military Manpower Administration and military hospitals, maintain consistency in military service decisions, and eradicate military service corruption by operating a two-tier system for military service exemption decisions. [1055] Experience and Section 3 The Korean War and National Defense The significance and historical lessons of the Korean War The Korean War was the first war in which North Korea blocked the intentions of the Soviet Union and China at a time when the Soviet Union was beginning to seize power after World War II

[1056] At this time, the United Nations dispatched the US military, and about 20 countries. However, the Korean War caused damage to 4 million families and 23 billion dollars in property during the war, solidifying the division of the nation until today, the 50th anniversary of the outbreak. We must understand this security reality, and

[1057] ㆍ 25 War Memorial: The purpose of the 6 ㆍ 25 War 50th Anniversary Commemoration Project is to ensure that the 6 ㆍ 25 War and the pole will never happen again on the land of the past. The military data of the 6 ㆍ 95 War 50th Anniversary Commemoration Project is a multimedia material. The year of the Patriotic War is a month of leisure! : The expansionist greed for the enemy was raised. The world war to protect freedom was launched under the surprise attack of the communist forces on June 25, 1950. Five countries including India provided medical care, and 10 million people were separated, causing wounds and becoming the beginning of the war

[1058] I will have to teach you a private lesson

[1059] To inherit this significance and lesson, and to secure the honor and welfare policies for aging veterans, and to the post-war generation. To promote national security awareness, strengthen ties with the 21 countries that participated in the war, and enhance national credibility, thereby contributing to the reconciliation, unification, and prosperity of the nation in the 21st century

[1060] This commemorative project, which will continue from June 25, 2000, to July 27, 2003, the 50th anniversary of the outbreak of the Korean War and the signing of the Armistice Agreement, is being promoted under the theme of “From War to Peace, From Division to Unification” and is divided into 52 basic projects and 41 delegated projects of government ministries and agencies. In 2000, 39 basic projects and 36 delegated projects, including central commemorative events, were promoted

[1061] This project aims to promote national security awareness through a correct understanding of the Korean War and to pass on the significance and historical lessons of the Korean War to future generations

[1062] To this end, we plan to academically re-establish areas of the history of the Korean War that have not been systematically established, and to systematically establish a total of 29 tasks, including 16 tasks in the military field that have not been established and 13 tasks in non-military fields such as politics, economy, society, and culture, and publish them as an appendix to the history of the Korean War

[1063] In relation to this, in April 2000, domestic and foreign scholars from the United States, Japan, China, Russia, and other countries participated in a defense academic conference on the topic of “The Korean War and Peace on the Korean Peninsula.” In June, scholars from Korea, the United States, and Japan participated in an academic conference on the topic of “50 Years Since the Korean War: From Cold War Confrontation to Peaceful Coexistence,” thereby re-examining the historical significance of the Korean War

[1064] Also, to honor the patriotic spirit of our ancestors and to carry on their spirit, the '6/25 War

[1065] A 50th anniversary symbolic sculpture is planned to be erected at the War Memorial of Korea, and 625 war-related battle sites across the country are being continuously maintained and purified

[1066] In addition, we are promoting this project in conjunction with local tourist attractions to make it a tourist attraction and attract many citizens

[1067] This is a project to promote the honor of veterans and expand welfare policies by providing practical welfare benefits to veterans and their families who dedicated themselves to protecting the country during the Korean War, thereby enhancing the honor and pride of participation in the war. To this end, a letter of appreciation in the name of the President was sent to express gratitude and respect for the patriotic devotion of domestic veterans

[1068] The Minister of National Defense presented a gift of appreciation to 53 recipients of the Taegeuk Military Merit Medal and their bereaved families

[1069] In addition, in order to honor the veterans of each region, village, and school, we are promoting the creation of memorial tablets for the fallen soldiers in each village, putting the names of veterans on road signs, hanging nameplates on the homes of veterans, awarding honorary diplomas to veterans, and creating busts of veterans and patriots

[1070] In addition, we are carrying out various events such as visits to and invitations to veterans, visits to the wounded, search for veterans, and events to honor war heroes in each region

[1071] In addition, in order to return the remains of the patriotic spirits who died fighting to protect the nation in crisis to their bereaved families, an excavation project is being carried out at 29 locations, including major battlefields such as Dabudong and Angang

[1072] In the first half of 2000, 41 sets of full-body remains, 1,410 partial remains, and 2,361 personal effects were excavated in the Dabudong, Angang, and Gaehwa-san areas. Of these, 144 sets of remains of South Korean soldiers were buried in the Karipmoji Namgol Dang and 3 sets were buried in the National Cemetery, 1 set of remains of an American soldier was handed over to the United States, and 2 sets of remains of North Korean soldiers were buried in the Paju North Korean Military Cemetery

[1073] In the second half of the year, excavations of remains are being carried out in areas such as Geumseong, Baekseoksan, Piui Ridge, and Chungju

[1074] Meanwhile, the Ministry of Patriots and Veterans Affairs is revising the “Act on the Honor and Support of Persons with Meritorious Service to the Nation” to raise the basic pension and expand the disability grades in order to cultivate the honor and pride of veterans

[1075] In addition, the Act on Support for Veterans and Others and its enforcement decree have been revised to provide practical welfare benefits, such as additional livelihood support, burial support, funeral assistance, and expanded medical benefits, to elderly veterans facing financial difficulties

[1076] This project aims to establish a national view and promote national security awareness among the post-war generation, which accounts for over 70% of the population, by vividly conveying the significance and historical lessons of the Korean War and fostering a harmonious national view and national security awareness

[1077] To this end, we cooperated with relevant organizations to ensure that relevant content was reflected in the elementary, middle, and high school curricula. [1081] This project aims to enhance Korea's national image and strengthen security ties by expressing sincere gratitude to the allies and veterans who fought alongside us when we were in crisis

[1084] This event also included the dedication of the 'Monument to the Fallen' of 38,000 UN soldiers and 30,000 ROK soldiers, and the opening ceremony of the 'Special Exhibition on the Korean War', thereby highlighting the historical significance of the Korean War and serving as an opportunity to enhance the nation's credibility among the participating countries and veterans

[1092] Section 4 The era of national defense, in which civilian experts participate in the national defense policy decision-making process, is possible only when the interest and participation of the entire public in national defense policy are heightened

[1093] To achieve this, defense policies must be established and implemented transparently, and established policies must be truthfully communicated to the public

[1094] As part of this, it is essential and a very effective method to involve civilian experts from various fields in the national defense decision-making process

[1095] The Ministry of National Defense is implementing the following various measures to encourage the participation of civilian experts and collect diverse opinions when establishing and deciding on defense policies

[1096] Since 1981, the Ministry of National Defense has been operating the 'Defense Policy Advisory Committee' composed of civilian experts to enhance mutual understanding between the civilian and military regarding national defense affairs

[1097] The National Defense Policy Advisory Committee is an organization that receives advice on major defense policies from advisory members with extensive knowledge and experience from academia and the media. It has 12 subcommittees: policy, finance, public relations/public information, planning and budget, legal affairs, personnel/welfare, mobilization, logistics, acquisition, facilities, and information system, and a total of 44 members are appointed and active

[1098] Policy advisory committee members participate in a plenary meeting held once a year and a special meeting once a quarter, chaired by the Minister, and provide advice on urgent matters through individual meetings, etc

[1099] In 1999, he advised on a total of 16 policies, including the evaluation of the construction of wastewater treatment facilities, and the '<40178 (Korean Navy Tactical Data System) Ji 0 1 06 Field Expert Ham: Hwi ㆍ Control Project', and in 3000, he advised on 'The feasibility of limiting possession when reducing the military power of the South and the North during the Peace Corps period

[1100] Going forward, the Ministry of National Defense plans to actively utilize advisory committee members to proactively respond to the rapidly changing domestic and international situations. In the past, participation of civilian experts in defense policy decisions was achieved indirectly through methods such as collecting opinions through surveys, utilizing research results as policy-making data, and exchanging opinions through seminars

[1101] However, recently, the Ministry of National Defense has formed a committee of civilian experts and is directly involving them in policy making and decision-making

[1102] In 1997, civilian experts were appointed as advisory and review committee members for the Defense Policy Review Committee, in 1998 for the Defense Reform Promotion Committee, and in 1999 for the Military Innovation Planning Group

[1103] The "Military Innovation Planning Group" is appointing and utilizing experts within the military and civilian experts in a full-fledged manner to collect and disseminate various innovative ideas, starting from the “Inban Social Motif”

[1104] We encourage and support defense-related academic seminars to encourage civilians to have a continuous interest in defense issues, and use them as important reference materials for their review and decision-making

[1105] The research was conducted exclusively using government subsidies, and was conducted by defense-related research institutes such as the Korea Institute for Defense Analyses and private organizations

[1106] Through competition like this, we are improving the quality of research and expanding opportunities for participation

[1109] The military is gradually operating and environmentally related to the need for an abnormal environment. [1110] We are implementing advanced education and in the future, we will establish a maritime system to train environmental specialists through advanced education in engineering and other fields. 2000 is a great success

[1111] Environmental education for soldiers is being carried out through military training institutions at all levels and through self-study training, etc., and instructors are invited from the Ministry of Environment, each regional environmental management office, local governments, and various environmental organizations, and a lot of emphasis is placed on the environment in commander opportunity training utilizing ideological training hours, etc. Meanwhile, the military is gradually improving the military environmental education system based on the 'Military Environmental Education Standards and Program Development' studied in 1999 to develop environmental education, and also plans to develop and utilize environmental education teaching materials suitable for the characteristics of the military

[1112] The government-military environmental council, which was established and operated in 1998, has one central council and four regional councils, each with a general water system and six regional councils in the Daegu and Wonju areas. They actively cooperate through four to five regular meetings and ad hoc consultations for environmental improvement of the military and regions

[1113] As part of the environmental council's work, we are strengthening mutual cooperation to preserve the natural ecosystem, including supporting environmental restoration activities at Daeamsan Yongnyeot and Baekdudaegan, and ecosystem surveys at the Mindongseon Station. We are also conducting joint government-military meetings several times a year, including environmental purification activities in the Han River basin

[1114] The military is also addressing issues such as the disposal of military waste collected from military ports, and the Ministry of Environment and local governments are providing the military with necessary materials, instructors, pollution measurement personnel, and equipment
